===== [p51-51] MACHINE DESCRIPTION > SERVO CONTROL LOOP (4块) =====
--[p51]-- The resolver is a rotary device that receives excitation signals from the digitizing reference module in the card cage and provides a feedback signal to the microprocessor digitizing module. The microprocessor digitizing module converts the feedback signal to digital position information to be used by the control software. The software routines compare actual motion to desired motion and provide a command value to the digital to analog (D/A) converter.
--[p51]-- The D/A converter provides a DC voltage (0 to +10 volts) proportional to the command value.
This command voltage is connected to the servo amplifier. The servo amplifier has two separate modules, each of which drives one axis. The amplifier produces a pulse width modulated signal proportional to the command voltage to drive the motor at the correct speed. A DC tachometer coupled to the servo motor produces a DC voltage proportional to motor speed.
--[p51]-- SERVO CONTROL LOOP
The machine contains two closed loop servo axes. One axis controls the motion of the work chuck carriage and is designated as the X axis (Axis 1). The other axis controls the feed mechanism for the grind spindle and is designated as the Z axis (Axis 2). Each axis contains several critical components required to perform the motion control functions.
--[p51]-- This tachometer is connected directly to the servo amplifier to provide tachometer loop compensation for stabilizing the velocity loop of the motor/drive circuit.

===== [p31-31] APPENDIX TO LOCKOUT SAFETY PROCEDURE: > Introduction (5块) =====
--[p31]-- Introduction
On October 30, 1989, the Lockout/Tagout Standard, 29 CFR 1910.147, went into effect. It was created to help reduce the death and injury rate caused by the unexpected energization or startup of machines, or the release of stored energy. The standard covers the servicing and maintenance of machines and equipment in which the unexpected energization, start-up or release of stored energy could cause injury [29 CFR 1910.147 (a)(1)(i), 1910.147 (a)(2)(I)].
--[p31]-- The lockout device shall be used unless the employer can demonstrate that the utilization of a tagout system will provide full employee protection. The tagout device shall be non-reusable, attached by hand, self-locking, and non-releasing with a minimum unlocking strength of no less than 50 pounds and must be at least equivalent to an allenvironment tolerant nylon cable tie.
--[p31]-- The lockout device must be substantial enough to prevent removal without use of excessive force or unusual techniques.
Tagout is the placement of a tagout device (a tag or other prominent warning device and a means of attachment) on an energy isolation device to indicate that the energy isolating device and the equipment being controlled may not be operated until the tagout device is removed.
--[p31]-- Normal production operations, cords and plugs under exclusive control, and hot tap operations are not covered [29 CPR 1910.147 (a)(2)(ii)]. This is intended to apply to energy sources such as electrical, mechanical, hydraulic, chemical, nuclear and thermal.
--[p31]-- Lockout is the placement of a lockout device on an energy isolation device (circuit breaker, slide gate, line valve, disconnect switch, etc.) to ensure that the energy isolating device and equipment being controlled cannot be operated until the lockout device is removed. A lockout device utilizes a positive means such as a lock (key or combination type) to hold an energy isolating device in a safe position and prevent the energization of a machine or equipment.

===== [p52-52] MACHINE DESCRIPTION > 24 VDC OUTPUTS (3块) =====
--[p52]-- When the output transistor is turned on the load is energized. Conversely, for sinking outputs the plus side of the load is connected to +24 VDC. The minus side of the load is connected to the positive output terminal. The negative output terminal is connected to DC common.
Refer to the Giddings & Lewis manual for a more complete description and schematic of the output modules.
--[p52]-- 24 VDC OUTPUTS
The control uses 24 VDC outputs for controlling solenoid actuated air and vacuum valves, relays, keypads, etc. The output consists of an optically isolated phototransistor that acts as a current switch to energize and de-energize the output load.
Outputs on the machine are both sourcing and sinking.
--[p52]-- For sourcing outputs the positive (collector) terminal of the output transistor (positive terminals correspond to pins identified by numbers on the output card edge connector) is connected to +24 VDC and the output load is connected to the minus (emitter) terminal (minus terminals correspond to pins identified by letters on the output card edge connector) of the output transistor. The minus side of the load is connected to DC common.

===== [p66-66] MECHANICAL OVERVIEW > CONTROL PANEL (2块) =====
--[p66]-- CONTROL PANEL
The machine control panel provides the necessary indicators, displays and controls to allow the operator to monitor and control critical machine functions. The basic features of the control panel are explained below.
1) CRT display - used to display machine control and operating information as instructed by the control program.
2) Keyboard - provides operator interface to machine control computer for data entry and machine control.
--[p66]-- 3) Digital tachometers (3) - displays angular speed of motors in R.P.M.
4) Potentiometers (6) - used to adjust motors to desired operating speed.
5) Pressure gauges (2) - indicate air pressure (P.S.I.G.) and vacuum (IN.HG.) present at respective inlets in back of machine.
6) Keyswitch - used to control operating level of machine (auto, manual, program).
7) Emergency Stop switch - safety switch to turn off all electrical power and stop machine in event of an emergency.
8) Audio alarm - beeper to notify operator of machine or cycle error conditions.

===== [p39-39] GALLIUM ARSENIDE AND GERMANIUM USE > Reactivity Warnings (2块) =====
--[p39]-- Reactivity Warnings
When heated, germanium is incompatible with bromine, chlorine, fluorine, oxygen, potassium chloride, and potassium. Additionally, mixing with aqua regia, concentrated nitric or sulfuric acids, fused alkalies, nitrates or carbonates, halogens, and oxidants should be avoided. When germanium decomposes, germanium dioxide is formed.
WARNING: Germanium may be a fire hazard if ground to a fine powder.
--[p39]-- When germanium is in this state, it ignites and burns on contact in chlorine or bromine.
WARNING: Do not heat germanium with potassium chlorate or potassium nitrate. An explosive reaction may occur. A violent reaction may occur with nitric acid.
WARNING: When heated with oxygen or potassium hydroxide, an incandescent reaction occurs.
See Section 6 of the germanium MSDS for additional information.

At a minimum, the following items should be worn when handling gallium arsenide: lab coat and apron, flame and chemical resistant coveralls, rubber protective gloves, and ANSI approved safety goggles.

===== [p65-65] MECHANICAL OVERVIEW > MACHINE CYCLE SEQUENCE WITHOUT WAFER TRANSPORT SYSTEM (5块) =====
--[p65]-- With the wafer held in place on the work chuck push both buttons simultaneously to start the grind cycle. The chuck will begin rotating and move in under the grind wheels to perform the programmed grind cycle. When the grind cycle is complete the chuck will return to the home position and continue rotating. The rinse valve will turn on to rinse the wafer. A brush may be used to clean the wafer at this time if desired. Push the right button to stop the chuck rotation and rinse.
--[p65]-- The measurement will be displayed on the CRT display and any required correction will be applied as with the automatic machine.
Pushing the left button will always pull vacuum on the work chuck to begin another grind cycle.
--[p65]-- MACHINE CYCLE SEQUENCE WITHOUT WAFER TRANSPORT SYSTEM
The manual version of the 7AA contains identical hardware used for the wafer grinding operation as the automatic version. However, the wafer transport system, including send elevators, centering station, pick and place assembly, rinse/spin station, and receive elevators is omitted. A measuring station is provided to maintain the closed loop thickness control feature.
--[p65]-- Also, an additional rinse spout is provided for rinsing the wafer manually after grinding.
Two palm buttons (left and right) are provided for operator control of the machine. The manual grind cycle sequence is explained below.
The operator first places the wafer on the ceramic work chuck. The wafer should be centered as close as possible for a symmetrical grind pattern. Push the left button to turn on vacuum at the work chuck.
--[p65]-- The ceramic chuck will be backflushed to "float" the wafer for easy removal.
Push the right button a second time to restart the chuck rotation and rinse. This allows for rinsing the chuck surface and manually washing the wafer at the second rinse spout. Push the right button a third time to stop the rotation and rinse.
When the wafer is placed on the measuring station chuck the probe will automatically raise to measure the wafer.

===== [p54-55] MECHANICAL OVERVIEW > GRIND SPINDLE SUPPORT (2块) =====
--[p55]-- The grind spindle itself slides within a precision honed bore located in a casting affixed to the upright. This casting is secured to the upright with four bolts and a hinge pin to provide side-to-side alignment adjustment. The spindle bore is periodically lubricated by the automatic lubricator to maintain smooth stick-free spindle movement.
--[p54]-- GRIND SPINDLE SUPPORT
The grind spindle support provides the mounting structure for the grind spindle assembly. The structure extends vertically from the mounting surfaces located on the grind assembly bed. The front of the spindle upright casting fastens securely to a flexure
crossmember on the bed. The rear of the upright is secured by a nut affixed to the rear end of the bed. The forward-rear tilt of the spindle is adjusted by rotating the upright bolt through the nut.

===== [p72-72] STEP 1 - MACHINE POWER UP > STEP 2 - MACHINE INITIALIZATION (3块) =====
--[p72]-- With the machine operation menu displayed, turn the key switch to "auto" and press the "2" key. This will display the "initialize machine" menu.

place cassette with test wafer on send elevator
place empty cassette on receive elevator
Cassettes can be placed on either send or receive elevator. Once both cassettes are in place the display will read:
close covers then press [start]

--[p72]-- STEP 2 - MACHINE INITIALIZATION
ÀDANGER
MOVING COMPONENTSCAN CRUSH BODY PARTS
keep hands and arms away from elevators, spindles, turrets, robots.
Machine initialization is a self-contained routine designed to perform machine calibration with minimal operator intervention. The initialization routine performs three basic functions: 1) find home position for work chuck, 2) find position of coarse grinding wheel, and 3) find position of finish grinding wheel.
--[p72]-- The machine control "learns" these positions and keeps track of all subsequent moves electronically. The machine loses this position data if machine power is turned off. Therefore, machine initialization is required following power-up in order to perform a grind cycle. Also, if any manual jog functions are performed before machine initialization the position data is meaningless and should be ignored.
To display the machine operation menu, press the reset key.

===== [p143-143] PNEUMATIC REGULATOR ADJUSTMENT (4块) =====
--[p143]-- PNEUMATIC REGULATOR ADJUSTMENT
ÀDANGER
MOVING COMPONENTSCAN CRUSH BODY PARTS
keep hands and arms away from elevators, spindles, turrets, robots.
Several pneumatic regulators are located on the machine. This section defines the location, function, and recommended operating level for these regulators.
As mentioned above the incoming pressure regulator is located between the primary 5 micron filter and coalescing filter.
--[p143]-- Newer model machines are equipped with several integral regulators mounted between the valve body and manifold. These regulators control the operating pressure for the individual valve. The regulators are input regulators, that is, they regulate the supply pressure for the valve supply port.
The pick and place clockwise regulator (SOL6) typical operating pressure is 50-60 PSI. A gauge on the gauge manifold above the valve manifold indicates the regulator setting.
--[p143]-- Assuming a maximum 10 PSI pressure drop across the coalescing filter and each air bearing filter pair, this regulated pressure should be 90 PSI minimum. If supply pressure does not exceed 100 PSI turn the regulator adjustment fully clockwise (viewed from below) to maximize the regulated pressure.
The regulator for the lubricated air valve manifold is located on the lubricator outlet port. This provides a stable supply pressure for the air valves. Typical operating pressure is 50-60 PSI.
--[p143]-- Regulators on valves 7-9 (SOL7-SOL9) regulate the up and down pressure for moving pick and place 1, 2 and 3, respectively. Typical operating pressure is 10-30 PSI.
Regulators on valves 10-14 (SOL11-SOL14) regulate the blowoff pressure for pick and place 1, 2 and 3 and the spin chuck, respectively. Typical operating pressure is 10-20 PSI.

===== [p101-101] MECHANICAL MAINTENANCE PROCEDURES > GRIND OPERATIONPROCEDURES (1块) =====
--[p101]-- GRIND OPERATIONPROCEDURES
There are many adjustment, calibration and compensation procedures that play an important role in maintaining optimal performance of the wafer grinding operation. This section provides a description of these procedures and how to implement them for maximum results.

===== [p84-85] MACHINE SETUP > data entry [ +0] (5块) =====
--[p84]-- data entry [ +0]
Enter the new data and verify that the number in brackets is correct. Press "ENT." The new data should now be displayed next to the corresponding parameter. If data is incorrect, repeat the above procedure using the correct data. The necessary data is explained below:
1) Coarse wear correction - enter the thickness in microns to compensate for coarse wheel wear.
--[p84]-- The computer will compensate the coarse grind wheel by this amount each time an accumulated thickness equal to the coarse wear limit is reached.
2) Coarse wear limit - enter the accumulated thickness in microns to prompt coarse wheel wear correction by the computer, this compensation is made after cumulative stock removal for wafers ground equals or exceeds the coarse wear limit.
3) Finish wear correction - enter the thickness in microns to compensate for finish wheel wear.
--[p85]-- This parameter is analogous to item 1 above but applied to the finish grind cycle.
4) Finish wear limit - enter the accumulated thickness in microns to prompt finish wheel wear correction by the computer. This parameter is analogous to item 2 above but applied to the finish grind cycle.
5) Test wafer thickness - this parameter displays the measured thickness of the last test wafer used for machine initialization.
--[p85]-- The error between actual thickness and desired thickness is the pre-travel thickness.
7) Dynamic offset - enter the thickness in microns to compensate for differences in dynamics of coarse and finish grinding wheels. This thickness should be set to eliminate any thickness error from a dual grind cycle after spindle pre-travel has been adjusted.
--[p85]-- 6) Spindle pre-travel - enter the thickness in microns to compensate for mechanical error affecting calibration. This thickness accounts for the difference between true grinding wheel height under static (calibration) and dynamic (grinding) conditions. This correction will provide more accurate finish thicknesses and is best determined by trial and error testing. To determine the pre-travel thickness, perform a single finish grind cycle.

===== [p33-37] GALLIUM ARSENIDE AND GERMANIUM USE > Health Warning (2块) =====
--[p33]-- Health Warning
Arsenic is a neurotoxin and a suspected skin and lung carcinogen. Poisoning may affect the heart, gastrointestinal system, kidneys, and liver. Its NFPA Rating for Health is 2 on a scale of 0 - 4.
--[p37]-- Health Warning
Germanium is generally considered to be low in toxicity. Its NFPA Rating for Health is 0 on a scale of 0 - 4.

===== [p133-133] OFFLINE STORAGE SUPPORT UTILITIES > WAFER CAPTURING UTILITIES 'F8' (3块) =====
--[p133]-- WAFER CAPTURING UTILITIES 'F8'
This mode enables the user to perform necessary functions to the data being captured during an auto cycle.
'A'ssign allows the user to assign a name to the file that the data is being stored. This should be done after the first wafer has been measured. The user will be prompted for "Lot #" data to be included in the file.
'I'nclude allows the user to include a 40 character comment in the file at any time.
--[p133]-- This command should be used for simple DOS commands like renaming or copying wafer data files for use by Lotus 123 or some other program. To access the DOS shell type 'ctrl' 'D' then the command desired. For example to get a directory of closed wafer data files type 'ctrl' 'D' dir \*.wdk 'enter'.
The PC screen will display the directory and then a message to strike any key when ready.
'Q'uit returns the user to the main menu.
--[p133]-- This could be used to note that the wheels were dressed or some other important event.
'C'lose causes the current file to be closed and the extension changed from ".WDT" to ".WDK".
Also available in this mode is shell to DOS to execute a DOS command. It should be noted however that wafer data capturing is disabled while executing a DOS command or program, and the Off-line Support Program is not terminated and is resident in memory.

===== [p54-54] MECHANICAL OVERVIEW > WORK SPINDLE CARRIAGE (1块) =====
--[p54]-- WORK SPINDLE CARRIAGE
The work spindle carriage consists of a cast iron casting. The carriage ways slide on the grind bed ways. This provides for smooth, stable travel of the work spindle assembly. The work spindle carriage assembly includes the work spindle air bearing and drive motor. The work spindle is driven with a belt coupling the motor and spindle pulleys. The carriage is driven by a ball nut mounted on the carriage.
The ball screw is fixed to the grind bed and driven by the x axis servo motor.

===== [p64-64] MECHANICAL OVERVIEW > PICK AND PLACE ASSEMBLY (2块) =====
--[p64]-- The mechanism for controlling the pick and place movement consists of two air cylinders and four reed switches to monitor its positions. Each P & P chuck uses vacuum and blow off for pick-up and removal of wafers, respectively. Vacuum sensors monitor the P & P chucks for reliable operation. Any pick and place errors will halt the auto cycle until cleared (see section 2.12).
--[p64]-- PICK AND PLACE ASSEMBLY
The pick and place assembly consists of 3 vacuum chucks mounted on a three-position rotating spindle. The 3 vacuum chucks are designated as P & P #1, P & P #2, and P & P #3. In the fully counterclockwise (ccw) position (as viewed from the top) the P & P #1 chuck is centered above the wafer pick-up station, the P & P #2 chuck is centered above the work chuck, and P & P #3 is centered above the rinse/spin chuck.

===== [p29-30] LOCKOUT SAFETY PROCEDURE > Sequence of Lockout Procedure (3块) =====
--[p29]-- Sequence of Lockout Procedure
1. Notify all affected employees that a lockout is required and the reason therefore.
2. If the equipment is operating, shut it down by the normal stopping procedure (depress stop button. open toggle switch, etc.).
3. For each energy source, operate the switch, valve, or other energy isolating device so that the energy source(s) (electrical, mechanical, hydraulic, etc.) is disconnected or isolated from the equipment.
--[p30]-- Stored energy, such as that in capacitors, springs, elevated machine members, rotating flywheels, hydraulic systems, and air, gas, steam, or water pressure. etc., must also be dissipated or restrained by methods such as grounding, repositioning, blocking, bleeding-down, etc.
4. Lockout the energy isolating devices with an assigned individual lock.
5.
--[p30]-- After ensuring that no personnel are exposed and as a check on having disconnected the energy sources, operate the push button or other normal operating controls to make certain the equipment will not operate.
CAUTION: Return operating controls to neutral position after the test.
6. The equipment is now locked out.

===== [p102-102] MECHANICAL MAINTENANCE PROCEDURES > ÀDANGER (2块) =====
--[p102]-- To remove the fine wheel use a $3 / 1 6 "$ hex wrench to loosen the six screws from the top perimeter of the mounting plate. To remove the coarse wheel loosen the four screws located on the inside of the wheel cup from below using a $5 / 1 6 "$ hex wrench. Install both wheels in the reverse order of removal.
Following replacement of either wheel it will be necessary to first true the wheel to the spindle and then to dress the wheel for grinding.
--[p102]-- ÀDANGER
BODY PARTS CAN BEENTANGLED AND CRUSHED
Stay clear of moving parts
Periodic inspection of the grinding wheels should be implemented to determine when wheels are worn and require replacement. Each wheel has $1 / 8 "$ of diamond bond when new. The bond will wear until the aluminum hub is exposed. Wheels will not grind once the aluminum has been exposed. Both wheels are fastened to the grind wheel mounting plate with stainless steel socket head cap screws.

===== [p24-24] GUARANTEE/WARRANTY CERTIFICATE > Notes: (1块) =====
--[p24]-- Notes:
Lifting the polish table(s) presents the potential for a lifting strain hazard; use the crane to avoid a strain. A table falling during the lifting process presents the potential of crushing body parts; keep body parts out from under the table at all times.
When moving the machine or associated equipment, always use a forklift. If the forklift is used from the front, confirm that forks extend to rear channel before moving.
If the forklift is used from either of the two sides, confirm that the forks extend to the center channel before moving.

===== [p131-131] OFFLINE STORAGE SUPPORT UTILITIES > TRANSFER GRIND PARAMETERS FROM DISK TO MACHINE 'F2' (1块) =====
--[p131]-- TRANSFER GRIND PARAMETERS FROM DISK TO MACHINE 'F2'
To transfer grind parameters stored in disk file to the Model 7AA memory, insure that the 7AA is at the main menu and press 'F2' on the PC.
Respond 'S' or 'D' to the parameter type to be transferred. You will then be asked for the filename to be used as the source of parameters. If the file exists respond to the prompt to continue or abort by pressing 'C' or 'A'.
If the 7AA is not in the proper menu an error message will be displayed and you will be given an opportunity to restart the transfer.

===== [p33-33] GALLIUM ARSENIDE AND GERMANIUM USE > GALLIUM ARSENIDE (GAAS) (1块) =====
--[p33]-- GALLIUM ARSENIDE (GAAS)
Gallium arsenide is used in the semiconductor industry in the form of polished wafers with a dark gray metallic sheen.
“Gallium arsenide technology (GaAs) III-V binary compound possesses good electrical properties, making this material highly attractive for electronic applications. In addition, GaAs possesses good and unique optoelectronic properties because it can be prepared in a semiinsulating state or behavior.”
\- Electronic Materials Handbook, ASM International,Vol.
1, pp. 199.

===== [p101-101] MECHANICAL MAINTENANCE PROCEDURES > SPINDLE SPEED SETTINGS (2块) =====
--[p101]-- SPINDLE SPEED SETTINGS
The rotational speeds of the spindles while grinding is critical to maximizing grind quality and wheel performance. Both grinding wheels consist of diamond particles suspended in a resinoid bond. Maximum grind spindle speed is 5000 RPM while the motor nameplate speed is 3450 RPM. Consult the factory for the optimum grind speeds for your wheels. Optimized grinding speeds reduce loading of the wheel and minimize wheel dressing frequency.
--[p101]-- The work spindle speed is not as critical as for the grind spindle. Maximum work spindle speed is 450 rpm. Consult the factory for optimum work chuck speeds.

===== [p21-21] GUARANTEE/WARRANTY CERTIFICATE > HAZARD MAGNITUDE LEVELS (1块) =====
--[p21 [表]]-- HAZARD MAGNITUDE LEVELS
The definitions of the keywords (signal words) used to define hazard levels are shown below:
Keyword | Definition
Danger - Immediate hazards which will result in severe personal injury or death.
Warning - Hazards or unsafe practices which could result in severe personal injury or death.
Caution - Hazards or unsafe practices which could result in minor personal injury or product or property damage.

===== [p62-62] MECHANICAL OVERVIEW > WAFER TRANSPORT SYSTEM (1块) =====
--[p62]-- WAFER TRANSPORT SYSTEM
The machine uses two send elevators and two receive elevators coupled with a three-arm two-position pick and place assembly to provide efficient, continuous movement of wafers through the cycle. The transport system contains the mechanics and necessary motors, switches and sensors to perform its operation. The logic for the transport system resides in the main machine control.

===== [p148-148] AIR BEARING SPINDLE MAINTENANCE > D.I. WATER FLOW VALVES (2块) =====
--[p148]-- D.I. WATER FLOW VALVES
DANGER
MOVING COMPONENTSCAN CRUSH BODY PARTS
keep hands and arms away from elevators, spindles, turrets, robots.
Three PVC needle valves are provided for adjusting water flow for each D.I. water solenoid valve. The flow valves are mounted on an angle bracket just below the grind drain pan. The valves will maintain an adjusted stream at the regulated D.I. water pressure.
The valve arrangement is as follows as viewed from the main cabinet door opening:
--[p148]-- upper left - fine wheel coolant
upper right - coarse wheel coolant
lower left - work chuck rinse
Rotating the adjustment knob clockwise will reduce the flow through each valve.
Both wheel coolant valves should always be fully open (counterclockwise) for maximum coolant flow while grinding. The rinse valve should be adjusted such that the water stream strikes the center of the chuck. This will provide coverage of the entire chuck while rotating.

===== [p15-15] REVISION RECORD > ABOUT THE MODEL 7AA WAFER GRINDER (3块) =====
--[p15]-- ABOUT THE MODEL 7AA WAFER GRINDER
The Model 7AA Wafer Grinder is designed specifically for mechanical grinding to remove material from the backside (the side opposite the electronics) of semiconductor wafers to a desired thickness. A fully automated machine, the 7AA transports wafers from dual feed cassettes, precision grinds each wafer to thickness, rinses and dries each wafer, measures wafer thickness, and unloads the processed wafers into dual receive cassettes.
--[p15]-- The two step grind, with coarse and fine grit wheels concentrically mounted, may do both rapid stock removal and fine finishing in a single wafer handling operation. The 7AA’s antideflection construction features a heavy duty cast iron column and head rigidly secured to a steel weldment base which runs the length of the machine. Head adjustment provides simple alignment between wheel and work spindle, assuring flatness.
--[p15]-- Managed by a two-axis, closed loop programmable control, all operations are fully automatic.
Air bearing spindles drive the grinding wheel(s) and the wafer chuck. Required flatness, stock removal, and surface finish combinations can be achieved at maximum production rates with the built in option of single or two step grinding. The single step surface grind is used for rapid stock removal or fine finish, depending upon the grit size of the single diamond wheel.

===== [p14-14] REVISION RECORD > INTRODUCTION (1块) =====
--[p14]-- INTRODUCTION

===== [p79-79] STEP 1 - MACHINE POWER UP > STEP 4 - GRIND MODE SELECTION (2块) =====
--[p79]-- The CRT will display the current grind parameters for that mode as programmed by the operator. The top of the screen is reserved for displaying any error conditions.
The display lists rapid feed rate, rapid feed thickness, finish feed rate and finish grind thickness on the first line. The feed wafer thickness and dwell time are displayed below. The measured wafer thickness for the last wafer is displayed in brackets in the lower right corner of the screen.
Axis coordinates are displayed along the bottom of the screen.
--[p79]-- STEP 4 - GRIND MODE SELECTION
The grind mode select menu is used to select a single or dual grind cycle. After all grind parameters are valid the auto cycle can be initiated by selecting one of the two grind cycles.
To display the machine operation menu, press the "Reset" key. Turn the key switch to "Auto" and press the "4" key. This will display the grind mode select menu. This menu lists two options; 1) single grind mode and 2) dual grind mode. push the key corresponding to the desired mode.

===== [p34-37] GALLIUM ARSENIDE AND GERMANIUM USE > Skin Contact (2块) =====
--[p37]-- Skin Contact
If skin contact occurs, remove contaminated clothing and flood skin with large amounts of water. If irritation persists, seek medical attention.
--[p34]-- Skin Contact
If skin contact occurs, remove contaminated clothing and flood skin with large amounts of water. The following effects may result: skin irritation, dermatitis and possible skin sensitization, pigmentation, and/or hyperkeratosis. If irritation persists, seek medical attention.

===== [p132-132] OFFLINE STORAGE SUPPORT UTILITIES > DIRECTORY OF WAFER DATA FILES 'F6' (1块) =====
--[p132]-- DIRECTORY OF WAFER DATA FILES 'F6'
To display a directory of all wafer data files on the disk press 'F6' from the main menu. There are a number of pauses for you to examine the filenames.

===== [p63-63] MECHANICAL OVERVIEW > RECEIVE ELEVATORS AND CONVEYOR (1块) =====
--[p63]-- RECEIVE ELEVATORS AND CONVEYOR
There are two receive elevators on the machine designated "Receive Elevator $\# 1 "$ (elevator nearest center of machine) and "Receive Elevator #2" (elevator nearest front of machine). Upon power-up of the machine, both elevators will raise to their upper travel limit. Either or both elevator(s) may be loaded with a cassette which is either completely or partially empty. The cassettes are serviced in the order that they are placed on the elevators.

===== [p128-128] SOFTWARE CALIBRATION PROCEDURES > Assumption (1块) =====

===== [p133-133] OFFLINE STORAGE SUPPORT UTILITIES > ACTIVE I/O STATUS UPDATE 'F7' (1块) =====
--[p133]-- ACTIVE I/O STATUS UPDATE 'F7'
To examine the contents of a variable or the status of an input/output or control relay press 'F7' from the main menu.
To examine the value of a variable enter 'V' with the number of the variable (0-899) desired.
To examine a new variable or change to a control relay press 'ctrl' 'C'.
To examine the status of an output, input, or control relay respond 'C after pressing 'F7' .
Then enter the number of the I/O point desired.
To return to the main menu press the 'esc' key.

===== [p54-54] MECHANICAL OVERVIEW (1块) =====
--[p54]-- MECHANICAL OVERVIEW
This section describes the fundamental principles of the mechanics of the grind operation for the 7AA. The mechanical functions consist of the grind assembly bed, work spindle carriage, grind spindle support, and grind spindle feed mechanism. The numerous other mechanical features of the machine are less critical and less complex and therefore should not require discussion here.

===== [p37-37] GALLIUM ARSENIDE AND GERMANIUM USE > GERMANIUM (GE) (1块) =====
--[p37]-- GERMANIUM (GE)
Germanium is used in the semiconductor industry in the form of polished wafers.
“Germanium crystals are grown in rods up to 1-3/8 in. (3.49 cm) in diameter for use in making transistor wafers. High-purity crystals are used for both P and N semiconductors. They are easier to purify and have a lower melting point than other semiconductors, specifically silicon.”
\- Electronic Materials Handbook, ASM International,Vol. 1, pp. 393.

===== [p34-37] GALLIUM ARSENIDE AND GERMANIUM USE > Eye Contact (2块) =====
--[p37]-- Eye Contact
Always wear chemical goggles or safety glasses as powder or dust may cause irritation. If eye contact occurs, immediately flush eyes, including under eyelids, with large amounts of water for at least 15 minutes. Call a physician for further instructions.
--[p34]-- Eye Contact
Always wear chemical goggles or safety glasses as powder or dust may cause irritation. If eye contact occurs, immediately flush eyes, including under eyelids, with large amounts of water for at least 15 minutes. Call a physician for further instructions.
